\section*{Priloga LOCO: celotna besedila desetih primerov}
Vse tri VLM ponovitve vseh treh modelov. Izvirne native naloge so ločene. Neveljaven JSON ostane neveljaven. Bajtno nespremenjeni surovi zapisi in tokeni so v 01-vlm, vse LLM ponovitve z razlogi pa v 02-llm in HTML. Nepodprti znaki v tem PDF so izrecno zapisani kot U+ kodne točke; izvirno besedilo ni spremenjeno v JSON/HTML.

\subsection*{loco-s1-33078 / florence / r1 / caption}
Status: ok. Call ID: a13db8d6e79a41e1884b863c1a2d600d. Napaka: --
\begin{quote}\footnotesize
</s>\allowbreak{}<s>\allowbreak{}The image shows a forklift truck in a warehouse. The forklift is orange and black in color and is positioned in the center of the image. It is facing towards the right side of the frame and appears to be moving through the warehouse. In the background,\allowbreak{} there are shelves filled with cardboard boxes and pallets. On the left side,\allowbreak{} there is a blue bin and a green sign with writing on it. The floor is made of concrete and the walls are painted white.</s>\allowbreak{}
\end{quote}

\subsection*{loco-s1-33078 / florence / r2 / caption}
Status: ok. Call ID: 05d5606cb59b4178a21b368f8aca15c9. Napaka: --
\begin{quote}\footnotesize
</s>\allowbreak{}<s>\allowbreak{}The image shows a forklift truck in a warehouse. The forklift is orange and black in color and is positioned in the center of the image. It is facing towards the right side of the frame and appears to be moving through the warehouse. In the background,\allowbreak{} there are shelves filled with cardboard boxes and pallets. On the left side,\allowbreak{} there is a blue bin and a green sign with writing on it. The floor is made of concrete and the walls are painted white.</s>\allowbreak{}
\end{quote}

Status: ok. Call ID: 2a1d0b9b20f24c42a8d7448d5e395e4e. Napaka: --
\begin{quote}\footnotesize
</s>\allowbreak{}<s>\allowbreak{}The image shows a forklift truck in a warehouse. The forklift is orange and black in color and is positioned in the center of the image. It is facing towards the right side of the frame and appears to be moving through the warehouse. In the background,\allowbreak{} there are shelves filled with cardboard boxes and pallets. On the left side,\allowbreak{} there is a blue bin and a green sign with writing on it. The floor is made of concrete and the walls are painted white.</s>\allowbreak{}
\end{quote}

\subsection*{loco-s1-33243 / florence / r2 / caption}
Status: ok. Call ID: 132bd008f2544192aa46c2bf7cdec964. Napaka: --
\begin{quote}\footnotesize
</s>\allowbreak{}<s>\allowbreak{}The image shows a large warehouse with multiple shelves and racks filled with boxes and other items. In the center of the image,\allowbreak{} there is a large metal cage with a green label on it. The cage appears to be made of metal wire mesh and is secured with a plastic wrap. On the left side of the cage,\allowbreak{} there are several cardboard boxes stacked on top of each other. The floor is made of concrete and there are two forklifts in the background. The warehouse is dimly lit with fluorescent lights.</s>\allowbreak{}
\end{quote}

\begin{quote}\footnotesize
</s>\allowbreak{}<s>\allowbreak{}The image shows a large warehouse with multiple shelves and racks filled with boxes and other items. In the center of the image,\allowbreak{} there is a large metal cage with a green label on it. The cage appears to be made of metal wire mesh and is secured with a plastic wrap. On the left side of the cage,\allowbreak{} there are several cardboard boxes stacked on top of each other. The floor is made of concrete and there are two forklifts in the background. The warehouse is dimly lit with fluorescent lights.</s>\allowbreak{}
\end{quote}

\subsection*{loco-s3-36694 / florence / r2 / caption}
Status: ok. Call ID: 61591976d65c4541b8be880d6049d0fd. Napaka: --
\begin{quote}\footnotesize
</s>\allowbreak{}<s>\allowbreak{}The image shows a large warehouse with a car parked in the center. The car is black and has its trunk open,\allowbreak{} revealing the interior of the car. A man is standing next to the car,\allowbreak{} loading boxes into a yellow pallet truck. The warehouse is filled with shelves and racks,\allowbreak{} and there is a forklift on the right side of the image. The floor is made of concrete and there are several boxes and pallets scattered around the warehouse. The ceiling is high and has multiple rows of lights.</s>\allowbreak{}
\end{quote}

\begin{quote}\footnotesize
</s>\allowbreak{}<s>\allowbreak{}The image shows a large warehouse with a car parked in the center. The car is black and has its trunk open,\allowbreak{} revealing the interior of the car. A man is standing next to the car,\allowbreak{} loading boxes into a yellow pallet truck. The warehouse is filled with shelves and racks,\allowbreak{} and there is a forklift on the right side of the image. The floor is made of concrete and there are several boxes and pallets scattered around the warehouse. The ceiling is high and has multiple rows of lights.</s>\allowbreak{}
\end{quote}

\begin{quote}\footnotesize
</s>\allowbreak{}<s>\allowbreak{}The image shows a large warehouse with rows of shelves and racks filled with cardboard boxes. The warehouse appears to be a large industrial space with high ceilings and bright lights. In the center of the image,\allowbreak{} there is a person walking through the warehouse,\allowbreak{} wearing a red jacket and black pants. The person is carrying a large red crate in their hand and is walking towards the right side of the frame. The image is taken from a low angle,\allowbreak{} giving a sense of movement and energy.</s>\allowbreak{}
\end{quote}
